# Agents · S · part 36

965 entries, solver to source-searcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **solver**: 구현 팀원 "번뜩" — 팀장(teamleader) 워크플로우의 깊이 담당. 하나의 어려운 문제를 끝까지 판다 — 핵심 슬라이스, 다른 팀원이 2회 실패한 버그, 통합·재현 불가·아키텍처급 버그. 병렬 물량은 맡지 않는다(그건 몽글). 팀장이 명시적으로 배정한 태스크만 수행. 사용자가 번뜩을 지명하거나 teamleader 스킬이 스폰/배분할 때만 사용. ([janjanjae/madang](https://github.com/janjanjae/madang/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/solver.md))

- **sonnet-job-visibility-engineer**: Convert hard rejection into resolution_flags and visible unresolved jobs. ([mjmichaelware/job-hunter-pro](https://github.com/mjmichaelware/job-hunter-pro/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sonnet-job-visibility-engineer.md))

- **sonnet-low**: Internal Kerd routing agent for Sonnet at low reasoning effort. Invoke only when Kerd Conductor or Agent explicitly selects kerd:sonnet-low for a delegated job; do not choose it for ordinary requests. The name carries the model and the effort so the running-job list shows both. The dispatching call still names the model (sonnet); this definition sets the same model, so a call that omits it still resolves to Sonnet, unless the host forces or restricts the subagent model. ([anthonymaley/Kerd](https://github.com/anthonymaley/Kerd/blob/HEAD/agents/sonnet-low.md) · ★3)
- **sonnet-low**: Nadir worker on Sonnet at low effort. Use for small scoped changes with an obvious approach: a one-function fix, a config change, a short test. - **sonnet-low**: Worker for simple, well-defined tasks with low ambiguity risk. ([dariovr1/sixth-sense](https://github.com/dariovr1/sixth-sense/blob/HEAD/agents/sonnet-low.md) · ★1)
- **sonnet-low**: Implementation agent on Sonnet at low reasoning effort. Builds an assigned slice of the Inkwell app, owning only the files it is told to own. - **sonnet-low**: Delegated worker (Sonnet) at low effort, for fully specified mechanical steps: searches, reading and summarizing named files, exact renames, listing, counting. - **sonnet-low**: Router worker: Sonnet 5.5 at low effort. Started only by the router. ([Light505/gswitchtool](https://github.com/Light505/gswitchtool/blob/HEAD/integrations/router-plugin/agents/sonnet-low.md))

- **sonnet-low**: Fast worker — Claude Sonnet at LOW reasoning effort. Give it ONE self-contained task with acceptance criteria; it works in its own worktree and reports a branch, not a merge. Opus/Fable remain the right choice for discovery, an unknown failure, correctness-critical verification and prose in the museum's voice — cheaper is not free when the brief needs interpreting.
